<div class="wrapper--grid align-center homepage--intro-text">
<div><p>Banff &amp; Lake Louise has a rich heritage as one of the world’s most awe-inspiring mountain destinations. With easy access to pristine wilderness, endless outdoor adventures, and all the amenities you need, in Banff National Park you feel truly immersed in nature.</p>
</div>

<h3>Plan the Ultimate Summer Vacation</h3> <div class="field field-name-field-link field-type-link-field field-label-hidden"><div class="field-items"><div class="field-item even"><a class="btn internal-link" href="https://www.banfflakelouise.com/summer">Learn More</a></div></div></div> </div>

<div class="feature-events" id="HomepageEvents"><header><h1 class="hdr-three">Featured Events</h1><p>There are events happening all year-round in Banff and Lake Louise. Be wowed and inspired by film screenings and talks from international writers and artists; celebrate the seasons with special events and performances; and dance under the stars at an outdoor concert. Have fun and seize the chance to experience something new with Banff and Lake Louise’s events offerings.</p>

absolute;top: 0;left: 0;width: 100%;height: 100%;\" width=\"853\"><\/iframe><\/div>\n\n\n\n<p>The annual SnowDays celebration\u00a0is a highlight of the winter season in Banff National Park; a free 12-day long event\u00a0held during January, centred around\u00a0massive snow sculptures in downtown Banff. Occurring at the same time as the <a href=\"\/ice-magic-festival\">Ice Magic Festival<\/a> in Lake Louise, you\u2019ll have plenty of winter creations to explore throughout the park.<\/p>\n\n<p><em>*Please note event details may change due to weather.<\/em><\/p>\n\n<!-- itinerary start -->\n<div class=\"itinerary itinerary--content_embed\" id=\"itinerary-1305\">\n  <div id=\"itinerary__section--1303\" class=\"itinerary__section itinerary__section--1  \"><div class=\"itinerary__section-contents\"><div class=\"itinerary__section-count\" aria-hidden=\"true\">01<\/div><h2 class=\"itinerary__section-title\">4 BEST PLACES TO VIEW SNOW SCULPTURES<\/h2><div class=\"itinerary__section-intro\"><p>Enjoy the festive atmosphere as downtown Banff transforms into a winter playground with giant snow\u00a0sculptures, ice skating, an ice and snow playground, and more! These are the best places to enjoy the sculptures in town and at the ski hills.\u00a0<\/p>\n<\/div><div class=\"itinerary__section-copy\"><h5>1. BEAR STREET<\/h5>\n\n<p>All the action of the International Snow Carving Competition will take place in the heart of Banff on Bear Street. Watch as expert snow sculptors create inspiring sculptures from January 17-19, or stroll the sidewalks to view the finished works of art during the rest of the festival.<\/p>\n\n<p><\/p><div class=\"media media-element-container media-natural\"><div class=\"embed\">\n<div id=\"file-2573\" class=\"file file-image file-image-jpeg\">\n\n      \n  <div class=\"content\">\n    <figure class=\"picture-wrap\"><!-- Begin Picture --><picture><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/l_1600_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=nvhDUNin 2x, https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=4_ng43NT 1x\" media=\"(min-width: 960px)\"><\/source><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=4_ng43NT \" media=\"(min-width: 480px)\"><\/source><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=4_ng43NT 2x, https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/s_480_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=KVvouYMu 1x\" media=\"(min-width: 0px)\"><\/source><img data-src=\"\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=4_ng43NT\" class=\"lazyload\" alt=\"Snow Days, Banff National Park\" \/><\/picture><\/figure><figcaption class=\"credit\"><span class=\"photographer\">Dan Evans<\/span><\/figcaption><\/div>\n\n<\/div>\n<\/div><!-- DEV: \/file_entity.tpl.php --><\/div>\n\n<h5>2. BANFF AVE SQUARE<\/h5>\n\n<p>A short walk from the main snow sculpting site, Banff Avenue Square is the next stop on your SnowDays itinerary and will also feature an\u00a0interactive exhibition of snow art.<\/p>\n\n<p><\/p><div class=\"media media-element-container media-natural\"><div class=\"embed\">\n<div id=\"file-2575\" class=\"file file-image file-image-jpeg\">\n\n      \n  <div class=\"content\">\n    <figure class=\"picture-wrap\"><!-- Begin Picture --><picture><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/l_1600_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-344_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=h7WGk1oB 2x, https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-344_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=Ktxw_02E 1x\" media=\"(min-width: 960px)\"><\/source><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-344_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=Ktxw_02E \" media=\"(min-width: 480px)\"><\/source><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-344_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=Ktxw_02E 2x, https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/s_480_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-344_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=24m9zfBr 1x\" media=\"(min-width: 0px)\"><\/source><img data-src=\"\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-344_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=Ktxw_02E\" class=\"lazyload\" alt=\"Banff Snow Days, Banff National Park \" \/><\/picture><\/figure><figcaption class=\"credit\"><span class=\"photographer\">Dan Evans<\/span><\/figcaption><\/div>\n\n<\/div>\n<\/div><!-- DEV: \/file_entity.tpl.php --><\/div>\n\n<h5>3. BANFF SUNSHINE<\/h5>\n\n<p>The celebration of winter continues at the ski hills. Head up to <a data-gtm_category=\"Activities\" data-gtm_event_value=\"105.0000\" data-gtm_label=\"Banff Sunshine Village\" data-gtm_no_track=\"N\" data-gtm_subcategory=\"Ski &amp; Snowboard\" MT. NORQUAY<\/h5>\n\n<p>Experience the best of winter, both on and off the slopes, at <a data-gtm_category=\"Activities\" data-gtm_event_value=\"65.0000\" data-gtm_label=\"Mt. The resort will\u00a0host a snow sculpture\u00a0in celebration of\u00a0the SnowDays festival.<\/p>\n\n<p><\/p><div class=\"media media-element-container media-natural\"><div class=\"embed\">\n<div class=\"itinerary__section-contents\"><div class=\"itinerary__section-count\" aria-hidden=\"true\">02<\/div><h2 class=\"itinerary__section-title\">Festival Tips<\/h2><div class=\"itinerary__section-intro\"><p>SnowDays\u00a0only comes around once a year, and with such an exclusive event you'll want to make sure you know all of the insider tips to make your visit as memorable as possible.\u00a0<\/p>\n<\/div><div class=\"itinerary__section-copy\"><h5>Stay in the Heart of Banff<\/h5>\n\n<p>Book an <a href=\"\/accommodation\">overnight accommodation in Banff<\/a> and explore all of the free winter activities and festivities.<\/p>\n\n<h5>Sample the SnowDays Tribute Beer<\/h5>\n\n<p>Head to <a data-gtm_category=\"Food &amp; Beverage\" data-gtm_event_value=\"1\" data-gtm_label=\"Banff Avenue Brew Pub\" data-gtm_no_track=\"N\" data-gtm_subcategory=\"Bar &amp; Restaurant\" data-gtm_trigger=\"website\" data-memberid=\"1989\" href=\"http:\/\/www.banffavebrewingco.ca\" target=\"_blank\">Banff Ave. Brewing Co.<\/a> to try the SnowDays Tribute Beer, a winter ale which will be on tap during the Festival.<\/p>\n\n<h5>Take Part in the Banff Gallery Hop<\/h5>\n\n<p>Immerse yourself in the unique arts, culture, and heritage traditions of Banff National Park on\u00a0the <a href=\"\/GalleryHop\">Banff Gallery Hop<\/a>.\u00a0<\/p>\n\n<h5>Get Your Skates On<\/h5>\n\n<p>If you are looking to rent ice skates, head to <a data-gtm_category=\"Retail\" data-gtm_event_value=\"1\" data-gtm_label=\"Snowtips-Bactrax Ski &amp; Bike Rental\" data-gtm_no_track=\"N\" data-gtm_subcategory=\"Sporting goods &amp; Apparel\" data-gtm_trigger=\"website\" data-memberid=\"2063\" href=\"http:\/\/www.snowtips-bactrax.com\" target=\"_blank\">Snowtips-Bactrax<\/a> on Bear Street.<\/p>\n\n<h5>Discover the Local Dining Scene<\/h5>\n\n<p>SnowDays is the perfect time to discover the rich culinary scene in Banff and Lake Louise. After a day of adventure in the snow, relax at one of the <a href=\"\/blog\/9-places-apres-banff-and-lake-louise\">10 Places to Apr\u00e8s\u00a0in Banff and Lake Louise<\/a>.\u00a0<\/p>\n\n<h5>Take Transit<\/h5>\n\n<p>The best way to get around town is to take transit with <a href=\"http:\/\/roamtransit.com\/schedules-routes\/banff-local-route-one\/\">four local routes<\/a> operating in Banff. absolute;top: 0;left: 0;width: 100%;height: 100%;\" width=\"853\"><\/iframe><\/div>\n\n\n\n<p>The annual SnowDays celebration\u00a0is a highlight of the winter season in Banff National Park; a free 12-day long event\u00a0held during January, centred around\u00a0massive snow sculptures in downtown Banff. Occurring at the same time as the <a href=\"\/ice-magic-festival\">Ice Magic Festival<\/a> in Lake Louise, you\u2019ll have plenty of winter creations to explore throughout the park.<\/p>\n\n<p><em>*Please note event details may change due to weather.<\/em><\/p>\n\n<!-- itinerary start -->\n<div class=\"itinerary itinerary--content_embed\" id=\"itinerary-1305\">\n  <div id=\"itinerary__section--1303\" class=\"itinerary__section itinerary__section--1  \"><div class=\"itinerary__section-contents\"><div class=\"itinerary__section-count\" aria-hidden=\"true\">01<\/div><h2 class=\"itinerary__section-title\">4 BEST PLACES TO VIEW SNOW SCULPTURES<\/h2><div class=\"itinerary__section-intro\"><p>Enjoy the festive atmosphere as downtown Banff transforms into a winter playground with giant snow\u00a0sculptures, ice skating, an ice and snow playground, and more! These are the best places to enjoy the sculptures in town and at the ski hills.\u00a0<\/p>\n<\/div><div class=\"itinerary__section-copy\"><h5>1. BEAR STREET<\/h5>\n\n<p>All the action of the International Snow Carving Competition will take place in the heart of Banff on Bear Street. Watch as expert snow sculptors create inspiring sculptures from January 17-19, or stroll the sidewalks to view the finished works of art during the rest of the festival.<\/p>\n\n<p><\/p><div class=\"media media-element-container media-natural\"><div class=\"embed\">\n<div id=\"file-2573--2\" class=\"file file-image file-image-jpeg\">\n\n      \n  <div class=\"content\">\n    <figure class=\"picture-wrap\"><!-- Begin Picture --><picture><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/l_1600_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=nvhDUNin 2x, https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=4_ng43NT 1x\" media=\"(min-width: 960px)\"><\/source><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=4_ng43NT \" media=\"(min-width: 480px)\"><\/source><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=4_ng43NT 2x, https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/s_480_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=KVvouYMu 1x\" media=\"(min-width: 0px)\"><\/source><img data-src=\"\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=4_ng43NT\" class=\"lazyload\" alt=\"Snow Days, Banff National Park\" \/><\/picture><\/figure><figcaption class=\"credit\"><span class=\"photographer\">Dan Evans<\/span><\/figcaption><\/div>\n\n<\/div>\n<\/div><!-- DEV: \/file_entity.tpl.php --><\/div>\n\n<h5>2. BANFF AVE SQUARE<\/h5>\n\n<p>A short walk from the main snow sculpting site, Banff Avenue Square is the next stop on your SnowDays itinerary and will also feature an\u00a0interactive exhibition of snow art.<\/p>\n\n<p><\/p><div class=\"media media-element-container media-natural\"><div class=\"embed\">\n<div id=\"file-2575--2\" class=\"file file-image file-image-jpeg\">\n\n      \n  <div class=\"content\">\n    <figure class=\"picture-wrap\"><!-- Begin Picture --><picture><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/l_1600_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-344_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=h7WGk1oB 2x, https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-344_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=Ktxw_02E 1x\" media=\"(min-width: 960px)\"><\/source><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-344_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=Ktxw_02E \" media=\"(min-width: 480px)\"><\/source><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-344_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=Ktxw_02E 2x, https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/s_480_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-344_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=24m9zfBr 1x\" media=\"(min-width: 0px)\"><\/source><img data-src=\"\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-344_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=Ktxw_02E\" class=\"lazyload\" alt=\"Banff Snow Days, Banff National Park \" \/><\/picture><\/figure><figcaption class=\"credit\"><span class=\"photographer\">Dan Evans<\/span><\/figcaption><\/div>\n\n<\/div>\n<\/div><!-- DEV: \/file_entity.tpl.php --><\/div>\n\n<h5>3. BANFF SUNSHINE<\/h5>\n\n<p>The celebration of winter continues at the ski hills. Head up to <a data-gtm_category=\"Activities\" data-gtm_event_value=\"105.0000\" data-gtm_label=\"Banff Sunshine Village\" data-gtm_no_track=\"N\" data-gtm_subcategory=\"Ski &amp; Snowboard\" carvings created at the ski resort.<\/p>\n\n<p><\/p><div class=\"media media-element-container media-natural\"><div class=\"embed\">\n<div id=\"file-573--2\" class=\"file file-image MT. NORQUAY<\/h5>\n\n<p>Experience the best of winter, both on and off the slopes, at <a data-gtm_category=\"Activities\" data-gtm_event_value=\"65.0000\" data-gtm_label=\"Mt. The resort will\u00a0host a snow sculpture\u00a0in celebration of\u00a0the SnowDays festival.<\/p>\n\n<p><\/p><div class=\"media media-element-container media-natural\"><div class=\"embed\">\n<div class=\"itinerary__section-contents\"><div class=\"itinerary__section-count\" aria-hidden=\"true\">02<\/div><h2 class=\"itinerary__section-title\">Festival Tips<\/h2><div class=\"itinerary__section-intro\"><p>SnowDays\u00a0only comes around once a year, and with such an exclusive event you'll want to make sure you know all of the insider tips to make your visit as memorable as possible.\u00a0<\/p>\n<\/div><div class=\"itinerary__section-copy\"><h5>Stay in the Heart of Banff<\/h5>\n\n<p>Book an <a href=\"\/accommodation\">overnight accommodation in Banff<\/a> and explore all of the free winter activities and festivities.<\/p>\n\n<h5>Sample the SnowDays Tribute Beer<\/h5>\n\n<p>Head to <a data-gtm_category=\"Food &amp; Beverage\" data-gtm_event_value=\"1\" data-gtm_label=\"Banff Avenue Brew Pub\" data-gtm_no_track=\"N\" data-gtm_subcategory=\"Bar &amp; Restaurant\" data-gtm_trigger=\"website\" data-memberid=\"1989\" href=\"http:\/\/www.banffavebrewingco.ca\" target=\"_blank\">Banff Ave. Brewing Co.<\/a> to try the SnowDays Tribute Beer, a winter ale which will be on tap during the Festival.<\/p>\n\n<h5>Take Part in the Banff Gallery Hop<\/h5>\n\n<p>Immerse yourself in the unique arts, culture, and heritage traditions of Banff National Park on\u00a0the <a href=\"\/GalleryHop\">Banff Gallery Hop<\/a>.\u00a0<\/p>\n\n<h5>Get Your Skates On<\/h5>\n\n<p>If you are looking to rent ice skates, head to <a data-gtm_category=\"Retail\" data-gtm_event_value=\"1\" data-gtm_label=\"Snowtips-Bactrax Ski &amp; Bike Rental\" data-gtm_no_track=\"N\" data-gtm_subcategory=\"Sporting goods &amp; Apparel\" data-gtm_trigger=\"website\" data-memberid=\"2063\" href=\"http:\/\/www.snowtips-bactrax.com\" target=\"_blank\">Snowtips-Bactrax<\/a> on Bear Street.<\/p>\n\n<h5>Discover the Local Dining Scene<\/h5>\n\n<p>SnowDays is the perfect time to discover the rich culinary scene in Banff and Lake Louise. After a day of adventure in the snow, relax at one of the <a href=\"\/blog\/9-places-apres-banff-and-lake-louise\">10 Places to Apr\u00e8s\u00a0in Banff and Lake Louise<\/a>.\u00a0<\/p>\n\n<h5>Take Transit<\/h5>\n\n<p>The best way to get around town is to take transit with <a href=\"http:\/\/roamtransit.com\/schedules-routes\/banff-local-route-one\/\">four local routes<\/a> operating in Banff. 12-day long event\u00a0held during January, centred around\u00a0massive snow sculptures in downtown Banff. Occurring at the same time as the <a href=\"\/ice-magic-festival\">Ice Magic Festival<\/a> in Lake Louise, you\u2019ll have plenty of winter creations to explore throughout the park.<\/p>\n\n<p><em>*Please note event details may change due to weather.<\/em><\/p>\n\n<!-- itinerary start -->\n<div class=\"itinerary itinerary--content_embed\" id=\"itinerary-1305\">\n  <div id=\"itinerary__section--1303\" class=\"itinerary__section itinerary__section--1  \"><div class=\"itinerary__section-contents\"><div class=\"itinerary__section-count\" aria-hidden=\"true\">01<\/div><h2 class=\"itinerary__section-title\">4 BEST PLACES TO VIEW SNOW SCULPTURES<\/h2><div class=\"itinerary__section-intro\"><p>Enjoy the festive atmosphere as downtown Banff transforms into a winter playground with giant snow\u00a0sculptures, ice skating, an ice and snow playground, and more! These are the best places to enjoy the sculptures in town and at the ski hills.\u00a0<\/p>\n<\/div><div class=\"itinerary__section-copy\"><h5>1. BEAR STREET<\/h5>\n\n<p>All the action of the International Snow Carving Competition will take place in the heart of Banff on Bear Street. Watch as expert snow sculptors create inspiring sculptures from January 17-19, or stroll the sidewalks to view the finished works of art during the rest of the festival.<\/p>\n\n<p><\/p><div class=\"media media-element-container media-natural\"><div class=\"embed\">\n<div id=\"file-2573--3\" class=\"file file-image file-image-jpeg\">\n\n      \n  <div class=\"content\">\n    <figure class=\"picture-wrap\"><!-- Begin Picture --><picture><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/l_1600_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=nvhDUNin 2x, https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=4_ng43NT 1x\" media=\"(min-width: 960px)\"><\/source><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=4_ng43NT \" media=\"(min-width: 480px)\"><\/source><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=4_ng43NT 2x, https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/s_480_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=KVvouYMu 1x\" media=\"(min-width: 0px)\"><\/source><img data-src=\"\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=4_ng43NT\" class=\"lazyload\" alt=\"Snow Days, Banff National Park\" \/><\/picture><\/figure><figcaption class=\"credit\"><span class=\"photographer\">Dan Evans<\/span><\/figcaption><\/div>\n\n<\/div>\n<\/div><!-- DEV: \/file_entity.tpl.php --><\/div>\n\n<h5>2. BANFF SUNSHINE<\/h5>\n\n<p>The celebration of winter continues at the ski hills. class=\"itinerary__section-contents\"><div class=\"itinerary__section-count\" aria-hidden=\"true\">02<\/div><h2 class=\"itinerary__section-title\">Festival Tips<\/h2><div class=\"itinerary__section-intro\"><p>SnowDays\u00a0only comes around once a year, and with such an exclusive event you'll want to make sure you know all of the insider tips to make your visit as memorable as possible.\u00a0<\/p>\n<\/div><div class=\"itinerary__section-copy\"><h5>Stay in the Heart of Banff<\/h5>\n\n<p>Book an <a href=\"\/accommodation\">overnight accommodation in Banff<\/a> and explore all of the free winter activities and festivities.<\/p>\n\n<h5>Sample the SnowDays Tribute Beer<\/h5>\n\n<p>Head to <a data-gtm_category=\"Food &amp; Beverage\" data-gtm_event_value=\"1\" data-gtm_label=\"Banff Avenue Brew Pub\" data-gtm_no_track=\"N\" data-gtm_subcategory=\"Bar &amp; Restaurant\" data-gtm_trigger=\"website\" data-memberid=\"1989\" href=\"http:\/\/www.banffavebrewingco.ca\" target=\"_blank\">Banff Ave. Brewing Co.<\/a> to try the SnowDays Tribute Beer, a winter ale which will be on tap during the Festival.<\/p>\n\n<h5>Take Part in the Banff Gallery Hop<\/h5>\n\n<p>Immerse yourself in the unique arts, culture, and heritage traditions of Banff National Park on\u00a0the <a href=\"\/GalleryHop\">Banff Gallery Hop<\/a>.\u00a0<\/p>\n\n<h5>Get Your Skates On<\/h5>\n\n<p>If you are looking to rent ice skates, head to <a data-gtm_category=\"Retail\" data-gtm_event_value=\"1\" data-gtm_label=\"Snowtips-Bactrax Ski &amp; Bike Rental\" data-gtm_no_track=\"N\" data-gtm_subcategory=\"Sporting goods &amp; Apparel\" data-gtm_trigger=\"website\" data-memberid=\"2063\" href=\"http:\/\/www.snowtips-bactrax.com\" target=\"_blank\">Snowtips-Bactrax<\/a> on Bear Street.<\/p>\n\n<h5>Discover the Local Dining Scene<\/h5>\n\n<p>SnowDays is the perfect time to discover the rich culinary scene in Banff and Lake Louise. After a day of adventure in the snow, relax at one of the <a href=\"\/blog\/9-places-apres-banff-and-lake-louise\">10 Places to Apr\u00e8s\u00a0in Banff and Lake Louise<\/a>.\u00a0<\/p>\n\n<h5>Take Transit<\/h5>\n\n<p>The best way to get around town is to take transit with <a href=\"http:\/\/roamtransit.com\/schedules-routes\/banff-local-route-one\/\">four local routes<\/a> operating in Banff. 12-day long event\u00a0held during January, centred around\u00a0massive snow sculptures in downtown Banff. Occurring at the same time as the <a href=\"\/ice-magic-festival\">Ice Magic Festival<\/a> in Lake Louise, you\u2019ll have plenty of winter creations to explore throughout the park.<\/p>\n\n<p><em>*Please note event details may change due to weather.<\/em><\/p>\n\n<!-- itinerary start -->\n<div class=\"itinerary itinerary--content_embed\" id=\"itinerary-1305\">\n  <div id=\"itinerary__section--1303\" class=\"itinerary__section itinerary__section--1  \"><div class=\"itinerary__section-contents\"><div class=\"itinerary__section-count\" aria-hidden=\"true\">01<\/div><h2 class=\"itinerary__section-title\">4 BEST PLACES TO VIEW SNOW SCULPTURES<\/h2><div class=\"itinerary__section-intro\"><p>Enjoy the festive atmosphere as downtown Banff transforms into a winter playground with giant snow\u00a0sculptures, ice skating, an ice and snow playground, and more! These are the best places to enjoy the sculptures in town and at the ski hills.\u00a0<\/p>\n<\/div><div class=\"itinerary__section-copy\"><h5>1. BEAR STREET<\/h5>\n\n<p>All the action of the International Snow Carving Competition will take place in the heart of Banff on Bear Street. Watch as expert snow sculptors create inspiring sculptures from January 17-19, or stroll the sidewalks to view the finished works of art during the rest of the festival.<\/p>\n\n<p><\/p><div class=\"media media-element-container media-natural\"><div class=\"embed\">\n<div id=\"file-2573--4\" class=\"file file-image file-image-jpeg\">\n\n      \n  <div class=\"content\">\n    <figure class=\"picture-wrap\"><!-- Begin Picture --><picture><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/l_1600_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=nvhDUNin 2x, https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=4_ng43NT 1x\" media=\"(min-width: 960px)\"><\/source><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=4_ng43NT \" media=\"(min-width: 480px)\"><\/source><source data-srcset=\"https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=4_ng43NT 2x, https:\/\/www.banfflakelouise.com\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/s_480_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=KVvouYMu 1x\" media=\"(min-width: 0px)\"><\/source><img data-src=\"\/sites\/default\/files\/styles\/m_960_natural\/public\/devans_20180120-244-_v1_2000x1333px.jpg?itok=4_ng43NT\" class=\"lazyload\" alt=\"Snow Days, Banff National Park\" \/><\/picture><\/figure><figcaption class=\"credit\"><span class=\"photographer\">Dan Evans<\/span><\/figcaption><\/div>\n\n<\/div>\n<\/div><!-- DEV: \/file_entity.tpl.php --><\/div>\n\n<h5>2. BANFF SUNSHINE<\/h5>\n\n<p>The celebration of winter continues at the ski hills. class=\"itinerary__section-contents\"><div class=\"itinerary__section-count\" aria-hidden=\"true\">02<\/div><h2 class=\"itinerary__section-title\">Festival Tips<\/h2><div class=\"itinerary__section-intro\"><p>SnowDays\u00a0only comes around once a year, and with such an exclusive event you'll want to make sure you know all of the insider tips to make your visit as memorable as possible.\u00a0<\/p>\n<\/div><div class=\"itinerary__section-copy\"><h5>Stay in the Heart of Banff<\/h5>\n\n<p>Book an <a href=\"\/accommodation\">overnight accommodation in Banff<\/a> and explore all of the free winter activities and festivities.<\/p>\n\n<h5>Sample the SnowDays Tribute Beer<\/h5>\n\n<p>Head to <a data-gtm_category=\"Food &amp; Beverage\" data-gtm_event_value=\"1\" data-gtm_label=\"Banff Avenue Brew Pub\" data-gtm_no_track=\"N\" data-gtm_subcategory=\"Bar &amp; Restaurant\" data-gtm_trigger=\"website\" data-memberid=\"1989\" href=\"http:\/\/www.banffavebrewingco.ca\" target=\"_blank\">Banff Ave. Brewing Co.<\/a> to try the SnowDays Tribute Beer, a winter ale which will be on tap during the Festival.<\/p>\n\n<h5>Take Part in the Banff Gallery Hop<\/h5>\n\n<p>Immerse yourself in the unique arts, culture, and heritage traditions of Banff National Park on\u00a0the <a href=\"\/GalleryHop\">Banff Gallery Hop<\/a>.\u00a0<\/p>\n\n<h5>Get Your Skates On<\/h5>\n\n<p>If you are looking to rent ice skates, head to <a data-gtm_category=\"Retail\" data-gtm_event_value=\"1\" data-gtm_label=\"Snowtips-Bactrax Ski &amp; Bike Rental\" data-gtm_no_track=\"N\" data-gtm_subcategory=\"Sporting goods &amp; Apparel\" data-gtm_trigger=\"website\" data-memberid=\"2063\" href=\"http:\/\/www.snowtips-bactrax.com\" target=\"_blank\">Snowtips-Bactrax<\/a> on Bear Street.<\/p>\n\n<h5>Discover the Local Dining Scene<\/h5>\n\n<p>SnowDays is the perfect time to discover the rich culinary scene in Banff and Lake Louise. After a day of adventure in the snow, relax at one of the <a href=\"\/blog\/9-places-apres-banff-and-lake-louise\">10 Places to Apr\u00e8s\u00a0in Banff and Lake Louise<\/a>.\u00a0<\/p>\n\n<h5>Take Transit<\/h5>\n\n<p>The best way to get around town is to take transit with <a href=\"http:\/\/roamtransit.com\/schedules-routes\/banff-local-route-one\/\">four local routes<\/a> operating in Banff.
